\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}=\ _{X}\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}
. So using this and (
9.5
), (
9.6
) becomes

\int dVT^{\alpha\beta}=-X^{\alpha}\int dV\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}T^{\mu\nu}+\frac{dX^{\alpha}}{dt}\int dVT^{\beta 0}+X^{\alpha}\int dV\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}T^{\mu\nu}=\frac{dX^{\alpha}}{dt}\int dVT^{\beta 0}.

(9.8)

To continue, we define the quantities for a monopole particle

M^{\alpha\beta}=u^{0}\int dVT^{\alpha\beta},

(9.9)

where
u^{\mu}
is the 4-velocity of the centre of mass of the particle with respect to the proper time,
u^{\mu}=\frac{dX^{\mu}}{d\tau}
so that
u^{0}=\frac{dt}{d\tau}
. Then, from (
9.5
) and (
9.8
), we have

\frac{d}{d\tau}\left(\frac{M^{\alpha 0}}{u^{0}}\right)+\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}M^{\mu\nu}=0\ \ \ \ ;\ \ \ \ M^{\alpha\beta}=\frac{u^{\alpha}}{u^{0}}M^{\beta 0}.

(9.10)

Taking the
\beta=0
equation, we have
M^{\alpha 0}=\frac{u^{\alpha}}{u^{0}}M^{00}
, and plugging this back in (
9.10
) we have

M^{\alpha\beta}=mu^{\alpha}u^{\beta},

(9.11)

where
m
, the rest mass of the particle, is given by
m=\frac{M^{00}}{(u^{0})^{2}}
. Plugging this in to the former equation in (
9.10
), we have that

\frac{d}{d\tau}\left(mu^{\alpha}\right)+\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}mu^{\mu}u^{\nu}=0.

(9.12)

Finally, by multiplying this equation by
u_{\alpha}
and using that
u_{\alpha}\frac{du^{\alpha}}{d\tau}+\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}u_{\alpha}u^{\mu}u^{\nu}=0
(since
u_{\mu}u^{\mu}=1
in this signature), we obtain 2 equations:

\frac{dm}{d\tau}=0,

(9.13)

\frac{du^{\alpha}}{d\tau}+\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}u^{\mu}u^{\nu}=0.

(9.14)

The first of these shows that the particle’s rest mass is conserved along its journey, while the second is the well-known geodesic equation that we expected to obtain. Next we repeat this calculation, but this time considering the dipole moments as well.

To start, we consider

\partial_{\delta}(x^{\alpha}x^{\beta}T^{\gamma\delta})=x^{\beta}T^{\gamma\alpha}+x^{\alpha}T^{\gamma\beta}-x^{\alpha}x^{\beta}\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}T^{\mu\nu},

(9.15)

where the last term was obtained using (
9.2
). For our dipole considerations, we will now use
\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}=\ _{X}\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}+\partial_{\sigma}(_{X}\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8. x^{\sigma}
but drop the

) to give

\frac{d}{dt}\int dVT^{\alpha dVT^{\mu\nu}+\partial_{\sigma}\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}\int dV\delta x^{\sigma}T^{\mu\nu}=0

(9.16)

and, recalling that
x^{\mu}=X^{\mu}+\delta x^{\mu}
, (
9.6
) gives

\int dVT^{\alpha\beta}=\frac{dX^{\alpha}}{dt}\int dVT^{\beta 0}+\frac{d}{dt}\int dV\delta x^{\alpha}T^{\beta 0}+\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}\int dV\delta x^{\alpha}T^{\mu\nu}.

(9.17)

Then, integrating (
9.15
) and using (
9.16
) and (
9.17
), after some calculations (wherein again we use similar tricks to before) we arrive at

\int dV\delta x^{\alpha}T^{\beta\gamma}+\int dV\delta x^{\beta}T^{\alpha\gamma}=\frac{dX^{\alpha}}{dt}\int dV\delta x^{\beta}T^{\gamma 0}+\frac{dX^{\beta}}{dt}\int dV\delta x^{\alpha}T^{\gamma 0}.

(9.18)

These are the equations of motion for the particle up to the dipole moment. We need to make things a bit more understandable, and to do so we begin by defining

M^{\lambda\mu\nu}=-u^{0}\int dV\delta x^{\lambda}T^{\mu\nu},

(9.19)

such that
M^{\lambda\mu\nu}
is symmetric in its last 2 indices, but not all 3. Also, since the integral is evaluated at a constant
t
,
M^{0\mu\nu}
is always 0. The spin tensor of the particle is closely related to this quantity, where the spin tensor is given by

S^{\alpha\beta}=\int dV\delta x^{\alpha}T^{\beta 0}-\int dV\delta x^{\beta}T^{\alpha 0}=-\frac{1}{u^{0}}(M^{\alpha\beta 0}-M^{\beta\alpha 0}).

(9.20)

Using the definition in (
9.19
), as well as the fact that
\frac{dX^{\alpha}}{dt}=\frac{u^{\alpha}}{u^{0}}
, we have from (
9.18
) that

u^{0}(M^{\alpha\beta\gamma}+M^{\beta\alpha\gamma})=u^{\alpha}M^{\beta\gamma 0}+u^{\beta}M^{\alpha\gamma 0}.

(9.21)

Let us call this the “
\alpha-\beta-\gamma
” version of this equation, based on the indices of the first
M
tensor. We can also get cyclic perturbations of this equation, and by adding the
\gamma-\alpha-\beta
equation and subtracting the
\beta-\gamma-\alpha
one, we obtain

2u^{0}M^{\alpha\beta\gamma}=u^{\alpha}(M^{\beta\gamma 0}+M^{\gamma\beta 0})-u^{0}(u^{\beta}S^{\alpha\gamma}+u^{\gamma}S^{\alpha\beta}),

(9.22)

where we also used (
9.20
). From (
9.21
) for the case where
\gamma=0
and (
9.20
) with
\beta=0
, we have that

u^{0}(M^{\alpha\beta 0}+M^{\beta\alpha 0})=u^{\alpha}M^{\beta 00}+u^{\beta}M^{\alpha 00}=-u^{\alpha}u^{0}S^{\beta 0}-u^{\beta}u^{0}S^{\alpha 0}.

(9.23)

Putting all the pieces together, and using that the spin tensor is antisymmetric, we have that

2M^{\alpha\beta\gamma}=\frac{u^{\alpha}}{u^{0}}(u^{\beta}S^{0\gamma}+u^{\gamma}S^{0\beta})-(S^{\alpha\beta}u^{\gamma}+S^{\alpha\gamma}u^{\beta}),

(9.24)

so that the
M^{\lambda\mu\nu}
can all be expressed in terms of velocities and spin tensors. Now we can rewrite (
9.17
) using (
9.9
) and (
9.19
) as

M^{\alpha\beta}=\frac{u^{\alpha}}{u^{0}}M^{\beta 0}-\frac{d}{d\tau}\left(\frac{M^{\alpha\beta 0}}{u^{0}}\right)-\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}M^{\alpha\mu\nu}.

(9.25)

Calculating the
\beta=0
term and substituting it back in, we have

M^{\alpha\beta}=\frac{u^{\alpha}}{u^{0}}\left(\frac{u^{\beta}}{u^{0}}M^{00}-\frac{d}{d\tau}\left(\frac{M^{\beta 0}}{u^{0}}\right)-\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}M^{\alpha\mu\nu}.

(9.26)

So, taking
M^{\alpha\beta}-M^{\beta\alpha}=0
owing to its symmetry, using the previous equation along with (
9.24
) and the
\beta=0
form of (

) and (
9.20
), we have the equation of motion for the spin tensor:

\frac{dS^{\alpha\beta}}{d\tau}+\frac{u^{\alpha}}{u^{0}}\frac{dS^{\beta 0}}{d\tau}-\frac{u^{\beta}}{u^{0}}\frac{dS^{\alpha 0}}{d\tau}+(\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}-\frac{u^{\alpha}}{u^{0}}\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{0}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{0}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt le$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}-\frac{u^{\beta}}{u^{0}}\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{0}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{0}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{0}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{0}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\d (9.27)

where we can use (
9.24
) to write the last two terms in terms of the spin tensor. We can also multiply (
9.16
) by
u^{0}
and rewrite it as

\frac{d}{d\tau}\left(\frac{M^{\alpha 0}}{u^{0}}\right)+\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}M^{\mu\nu}-\partial_{\sigma}\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}M^{\sigma\mu\nu}=0.

(9.28)

At this point, we have 10 unknowns:
M^{00}
, 3 independent components of the velocity vector, and 6 independent components of the antisymmetric spin tensor. At first glance, we appear to have 6 equations from (
9.27
) (owing to the antisymmetry in
\alpha\leftrightarrow\beta
) and 4 equations from (
9.28
), but unfortunately 3 of those in (
9.27
) are trivial identities
[
79
]
, and as such we will need supplementary conditions, to be chosen a bit later.

We wish to write our equations of motion in covariant notation. Firstly, it can be shown
[
79
]
that
S^{\alpha\beta}
is a tensor,
\frac{1}{u^{0}}\left(M^{\alpha 0}+\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}u^{\mu}S^{\nu 0}\right)
is a vector, and as such,

m=\frac{u_{\alpha}}{u^{0}}\left(M^{\alpha 0}+\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}u^{\mu}S^{\nu 0}\right)

(9.29)

is a scalar representing the mass of the particle, which has changed from the monopole particle which recall had
m=\frac{M^{00}}{(u^{0})^{2}}
.

Next, we define
\frac{Df}{D\tau}=u^{\nu}\nabla_{\nu}f
and note that
u^{\nu}\partial_{\nu}f=\frac{df}{d\tau}
. Writing (
9.27
) in terms of this covariant derivative, and using some symmetries of indices, we immediately have the first of the Mathisson-Papapetrou equations:

\frac{DS^{\alpha\beta}}{D\tau}=p^{\alpha}u^{\beta}-p^{\beta}u^{\alpha},

(9.30)

wherein
p^{\mu}=\frac{M^{\mu 0}}{u^{0}}-\frac{u^{\nu}}{u^{0}}\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.20804pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.20804pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.55505pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96788pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.20804pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.20804pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.55505pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96788pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.20804pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.20804pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.55505pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96788pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.20804pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.20804pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.55505pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96788pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\nu}}}}S^{0\alpha}
is the momentum of our dipole particle.

Using our covariant derivative now, we have from (
9.27
) a Bianchi-type identity for the covariant derivative of the spin:

\frac{u^{0}}{u^{0}}\frac{DS^{\alpha\beta}}{D\tau}+\frac{u^{\alpha}}{u^{0}}\frac{DS^{\beta 0}}{D\tau}+\frac{u^{\beta}}{u^{0}}\frac{DS^{0\alpha}}{D\tau}=0,

(9.31)

where
\frac{u^{0}}{u^{0}}
is obviously unity but was left in for symmetry. Using this, along with the fact that
u^{0}S^{\alpha 0}=-M^{\alpha 00}
, the
\beta=0
equation from (
9.25
) yields

M^{\alpha 0}+\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}S^{\mu 0}u^{\nu}=\frac{u^{\alpha}}{u^{0}}\left(M^{00}+\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{0}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{0}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{0}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{0}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}S^{\mu 0}u^{\nu}\right)+\frac{DS^{\alpha 0}}{D\tau}.

(9.32)

From this, and using the mass in (
9.29
) as well as (
9.31
), we find

\frac{M^{\alpha 0}}{u^{0}}+\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}S^{\mu 0}\frac{u^{\nu}}{u^{0}}=mu^{\alpha}+u_{\beta}\frac{DS^{\alpha\beta}}{D\tau},

(9.33)

which is equivalent to
p^{\alpha}
, as we saw above, and so we now have 2 expressions for the momentum of the particle. In usual flat space for a spinless particle, we would not have the second term on either side of this equation: it is interesting to note that the dipole nature of the particle causes a change to its momentum based on the coupling of curvature and spin.

Inserting this result into (
9.28
), and using all the results derived until this point along with the relationship between the Riemann curvature tensor and the Christoffel symbols, we obtain the second Mathisson-Papapetrou (M-P) equation:

\frac{Dp^{\alpha}}{D\tau}=\frac{1}{2}\mathchoice{R^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.406pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.406pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.14456pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81754pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta\mu\nu}}}}{R^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.406pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.406pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.14456pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81754pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta\mu\nu}}}}{R^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.406pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.406pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.14456pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81754pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta\mu\nu}}}}{R^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.406pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.406pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.14456pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81754pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta\mu\nu}}}}u^{\beta}S^{\mu\nu}.

(9.34)

The final piece of information we need comes in the form of the Dixon-Tulczyjew supplementary condition. To begin, we can rewrite our spin tensor in terms of the particle’s spin vector using
[
80
]

S^{\alpha}=\frac{1}{2}\mathchoice{\eta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\mu\nu}}}_{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.50754pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.0768pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\eta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\mu\nu}}}_{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.50754pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.0768pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\eta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\mu\nu}}}_{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.50754pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.0768pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\eta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\mu\nu}}}_{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.50754pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.0768pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}u^{\beta}S_{\mu\nu},

(9.35)

wherein
\eta_{\beta\alpha\mu\nu}=\sqrt{-g}\epsilon_{\beta\alpha\mu\nu}
and
\epsilon_{\beta\alpha\mu\nu}
is the 4D Levi-Civita tensor. The converse of this is that
S_{\mu\nu}=\eta_{\beta\alpha\mu\nu}u^{\beta}S^{\alpha}
.

From this relation, we can write the magnitude of the spin vector
s
in terms of the spin tensor:

s^{2}=S^{\alpha}S_{\alpha}=\frac{1}{2}S_{\mu\nu}S^{\mu\nu}.

(9.36)

For the case of a neutrino, we know that
s^{2}=\braket{S^{2}}=\tilde{s}(\tilde{s}+1)=\frac{3}{4}
(when
\hbar=1
) since neutrinos are spin-
\frac{1}{2}
particles. This quantity
s^{2}
is conserved, and as such we have that

\frac{D(s^{2})}{D\tau}=0=\frac{D}{D\tau}\left(\frac{1}{2}S_{\mu\nu}S^{\mu\nu}\right)=S_{\mu\nu}\frac{DS^{\mu\nu}}{D\tau}=S_{\mu\nu}(p^{\mu}u^{\nu}-p^{\nu}u^{\mu})=2S_{\mu\nu}p^{\mu}u^{\nu},

(9.37)

where we used (
9.30
) and the antisymmetry of the spin tensor. This gives the Dixon-Tulczyjew supplementary condition: that
S^{\mu\nu}p_{\nu}=0

25
25

25

This could give an alternate condition,
S^{\mu\nu}u_{\nu}=0
. There are in fact many different supplementary conditions that can be considered, but for our purposes we will focus on the D-T condition.
. This gives the final 3 equations needed to solve our system.

Thus, the full system of equations - known collectively now as the Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon-Tulczyjew equations - are

\frac{Dp^{\alpha}}{D\tau}=\frac{1}{2}\mathchoice{R^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.406pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.406pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.14456pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81754pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta\mu\nu}}}}{R^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.406pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.406pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.14456pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81754pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta\mu\nu}}}}{R^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.406pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.406pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.14456pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81754pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta\mu\nu}}}}{R^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.406pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.406pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.14456pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81754pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta\mu\nu}}}}u^{\beta}S^{\mu\nu},

(9.38)

\frac{DS^{\alpha\beta}}{D\tau}=p^{\alpha}u^{\beta}-p^{\beta}u^{\alpha},

(9.39)

S^{\mu\nu}p_{\nu}=0.

(9.40)

9.2
As a Result of QFT Considerations

Though classical and quantum “spin” have a tenuous relationship, and the comparison between them is often misguided, the M-P equations seem to provide a scenario wherein the classical spin - that is, the movement of a particle around its centre of mass - and the quantum spin - a quantum number conserved as an intrinsic property of a particle - align. Though this derivation will lead to (almost) the same equations, there are some caveats, as we shall discuss.

We begin with the Dirac equation in curved spacetime:

i\gamma^{\mu}\nabla_{\mu}\psi-\frac{m}{\hbar}\psi=0,

(9.41)

where the covariant derivative acting on a spinor is related to the orthonormal tetrad field
\mathchoice{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
via

\nabla_{\mu}=\partial_{\mu}+\frac{1}{4}\left(\nabla_{\mu}\mathchoice{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\right)h_{\alpha b}\gamma^{b}\gamma^{a}.

(9.42)

These orthonormal tetrad fields are related by
g_{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}_{{{b}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}_{{{b}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}_{{{b}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}_{{{b}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}=\eta_{ab}
, with
\eta_{ab}
the Minkowski metric. In this subsection, Greek indices will be raised and lowered using our spacetime metric, while the Minkowski metric applies to Latin indices. The factors of
\hbar
will be kept in explicitly, as this is the small parameter that we will perform perturbations around. For the sake of following the derivation in
[
81
]
, we once again work in the
(+,-,-,-)
signature.

In order to analyse the classical limit of our quantum particle, we expand its wave function in a WKB expansion, keeping the first two terms:

\psi(x)=e^{iS(x)/\hbar}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(-i\hbar)^{n}a_{n}(x)=e^{iS(x)/\hbar}(a_{0}(x)-i\hbar a_{1}(x)+\dots).

(9.43)

Inserting this into (
9.41
) and equating coefficients of powers of
\hbar
, we get for the
\hbar^{-1}
and
\hbar^{0}
equations

(\gamma^{\mu}\nabla_{\mu}S+m)a_{0}=0,

(9.44)

(\gamma^{\mu}\nabla_{\mu}S+m)a_{1}=-\gamma^{\mu}\nabla_{\mu}a_{0}.

(9.45)

In order for
a_{0}
not to be just 0 (such that our wave function is not the trivial solution), we require that
\det(\gamma^{\mu}\nabla_{\mu}S+m)=0
, so we can write
\gamma^{\mu}\nabla_{\mu}S=-m
. Multiplying this by
\gamma^{\nu}\nabla_{\nu}S
and using the anticommutation relation between gamma matrices

\gamma^{(\mu}\gamma^{\nu)}=g^{\mu\nu},

(9.46)

we have that
\nabla^{\mu}S\nabla_{\mu}S=m^{2}
. Owing to this, we define a momentum-like quantity
P_{\mu}=-\nabla_{\mu}S
, such that it satisfies the on-shell condition (
P^{2}=m^{2}
) and if the wave function has a plane-wave structure, then this is the momentum. Continuing this comparison, we define a velocity-like quantity
v_{\mu}=\frac{p_{\mu}}{m}
, which is normalised correctly:
v^{\mu}v_{\mu}=1
. It is difficult to apply this formalism to the case of a massless fermion, as there does not seem to be a candidate to act as a meaningful, correctly-normalised velocity. These
v^{\mu}
are simply timelike normalised vectors orthogonal to the spacelike hypersurfaces of
S=
constant that form a congruence of geodesic worldlines through each
S=
constant. The covariant derivative of this congruence can be decomposed into

\nabla_{\alpha}v_{\beta}=\sigma_{\alpha\beta}+\omega_{\alpha\beta}+\frac{\theta}{3}k_{\alpha\beta},

(9.47)

where
\frac{\theta}{3}k_{\alpha\beta}=\frac{\theta}{3}(g_{\alpha\beta}-v_{\alpha}v_{\beta})
is the expansion of the congruence
26
26

26

This
k_{\alpha\beta}
is usually denoted
h_{\alpha\beta}
but this could cause confusion with respect to the tetrad fields.
,
\sigma_{\alpha\beta}=\mathchoice{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\nabla_{(\mu}v_{\nu)}-\frac{1}{3}\nabla_{\mu}v^{\mu}k_{\alpha\beta}
its shear, and
\omega_{\alpha\beta}=\nabla_{[\mu}v_{\nu]}-\frac{D}{D\tau}(v_{[\alpha})v_{\beta]}
is the vorticity, which is 0 since these are geodesics. So
\nabla_{\alpha}v_{\beta}=\sigma_{\alpha\beta}+\frac{\theta}{3}k_{\alpha\beta}
.

Let us return to solving (
9.44
). We make the ansatz that
a_{0}(x)=\beta_{1}(x)b_{01}(x)+\beta_{2}(x)b_{02}(x)
, and using that

\gamma^{0}=\begin{pmatrix}\mathds{1}&0\\
0&-\mathds{1}\end{pmatrix}\ \ \ \ \ ;\ \ \ \ \ \gamma^{i}=\begin{pmatrix}0&\sigma^{i}\\
-\sigma^{i}&0\end{pmatrix},

(9.48)

where
\sigma^{i}
are of course the Pauli matrices, calculating similarly to (
7.18
), we have the orthogonal solutions

b_{01}=\sqrt{\frac{E+m}{2m}}\begin{pmatrix}1\\
0\\
\frac{k^{3}}{E+m}\\
\frac{k^{1}+ik^{2}}{E+m}\end{pmatrix}\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ b_{02}=\sqrt{\frac{E+m}{2m}}\begin{pmatrix}0\\
1\\
\frac{k^{1}-ik^{2}}{E+m}\\
-\frac{k^{3}}{E+m}\end{pmatrix},

(9.49)

where we have defined
E=p^{\mu}\mathchoice{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
and
k^{i}=p^{\mu}\mathchoice{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
.

The homogeneous part of (
9.45
) is the same as (
9.44
), and so
a_{1}
’s 3 solutions are
b_{01}
,
b_{02}
, and a final solution proportional to the inhomogeneity
\gamma^{\mu}\nabla_{\mu}a_{0}
. In order for this to be another solution and not merely a linear combination of the others, it must be orthogonal to them. Thus we have

\bar{b}_{0i}\gamma^{\mu}\nabla_{\mu}a_{0}=0,

(9.50)

where
i=1,2
and the bar denotes Pauli conjugation,
\bar{f}=f^{\dagger}\gamma^{0}
.

To continue, we can select a particular tetrad. We do not lose any generality in doing so, as tetrads can be later rotated to any general tetrad to retreive a general result
[
81
]
. We choose our tetrad such that

\mathchoice{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}=v^{\mu}\ \ \ \ ;\ \ \ \ \nabla_{\alpha}\mathchoice{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}=0.

(9.51)

In this particular case, we have
\nabla_{\mu}=\partial_{\mu}
,
E=m
and
k^{i}=0
. It is easy to see then that

\bar{b}_{0i}\gamma^{\mu}b_{0j}=v^{\mu}\delta_{ij},

(9.52)

since
\gamma^{\mu}={h}{{}^{\mu}_{a}}\gamma^{a}
. Similarly, in this particular tetrad, we have
(\nabla_{\mu}E){h}{{}^{\mu}_{a}}=0
as well as
(\nabla_{\mu}k^{i}){h}{{}^{\mu}_{0}}=0
, but
(\nabla_{\mu}k^{i}){h}{{}^{\mu}_{j}}=m{h}{{}^{\mu}_{i}}(\sigma_{\mu\alpha}+\frac{\theta}{3}k_{\mu\alpha}){h}{{}^{\alpha}_{j}}
, where we used (
9.47
).

Using this, as well as some properties of the shear (it is symmetric, traceless and orthogonal to
v^{\alpha}
), we arrive at

\bar{b}_{0i}\gamma^{\mu}\nabla_{\mu}b_{0j}=\frac{\theta}{2}\delta_{ij}.

(9.53)

Note that in this calculation, derivatives and products were calculated before taking the particular values for
E
and
k^{i}
. Now we wish to see what (
9.50
) becomes. Plugging in the ansatz for
a_{0}
and using (
9.52
) and (
9.53
), we have that

\partial_{\alpha}\beta_{i}v^{\alpha}+\beta_{i}\bar{b}_{0i}\gamma^{\mu}\nabla_{\mu}b_{0i}+\beta_{j}\bar{b}_{0i}\gamma^{\mu}\nabla_{\mu}b_{0j}=0\ \ \ \rightarrow\ \ \ \partial_{\alpha}\beta_{i}v^{\alpha}=-\frac{\theta}{2}\beta_{i},

(9.54)

where in the above
i\neq j
. Using that
(\nabla_{\mu}k^{i}){h}{{}^{\mu}_{0}}=0
, we also have that
\nabla_{\mu}b_{0i}v^{\mu}=0
.

We can now rewrite
a_{0}
as
a_{0}=fb_{0}
, where
b_{0}
is normalised (
\bar{b}_{0}b_{0}=1
) and
f^{2}=\beta_{1}^{*}\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}^{*}\beta_{2}
.
f
contains the expansion of the congruence. That is,

\partial_{\alpha}fv^{\alpha}=-\frac{\theta}{2}f\ \ \ \ ;\ \ \ \ \nabla_{\alpha}b_{0}v^{\alpha}=0.

(9.55)

The next part of the process involves performing a Gordon decomposition of the Dirac number current:

j^{\mu}=j_{c}^{\mu}+j_{m}^{\mu},

(9.56)

with
j^{\mu}=\bar{\psi}\gamma^{\mu}\psi
the particle number current,
j_{c}^{\mu}=\frac{\hbar}{2mi}\left(\nabla^{\mu}\bar{\psi}\psi-\bar{\psi}\nabla^{\mu}\psi\right)
acting analogously to a convection four-current and
j_{m}^{\mu}=\frac{\hbar}{2m}\nabla_{\nu}\left(\bar{\psi}\sigma^{\mu\nu}\psi\right)
analogous to a magnetisation four-current. It is not difficult to show (
9.56
) is true by use of the Dirac equation. This
\sigma^{\mu\nu}
is not the shear of the worldline congruence, but rather is defined by
\sigma^{\mu\nu}=i\gamma^{[\mu}\gamma^{\nu]}
.

We have
\nabla_{\mu}j^{\mu}=0
by the Dirac equation,
\nabla_{\mu}j_{m}^{\mu}=0
by symmetry arguments and thus
\nabla_{\mu}j_{c}^{\mu}=0
by (
9.56
).

Inserting our WKB expansion, we have up to order
\hbar

j_{c}^{\mu}=f^{2}v^{\mu}-i\hbar(\bar{a}_{0}a_{1}-\bar{a}_{1}a_{0})v^{\mu}-\frac{i\hbar}{2m}\left(\nabla^{\mu}\bar{b}_{0}b_{0}-\bar{b}_{0}\nabla^{\mu}b_{0}\right),

(9.57)

j_{m}^{\mu}=\frac{\hbar}{2m}\nabla_{\nu}\left(\bar{a}_{0}\sigma^{\mu\nu}a_{0}\right).

(9.58)

Next we can define a vector
u^{\mu}=\frac{1}{\mathscr{N}}j_{c}^{\mu}
as the normalised convection current. This also defines a congruence of timelike curves, and at zeroth order it is the same as
v^{\mu}
. This
u^{\mu}
can be interpreted as the velocity of the particles. Since we just need
u^{\mu}
to be normalised up to order
\hbar
, that is,
u^{\mu}u_{\mu}=1+\mathscr{O}(\hbar^{2})
, using (
9.55
) we can write

u^{\mu}=v^{\mu}+\frac{\hbar}{2mi}\left(\nabla^{\mu}\bar{b}_{0}b_{0}-\bar{b}_{0}\nabla^{\mu}b_{0}\right).

(9.59)

Similarly, we can define a tensor
S^{\alpha\beta}
via

S^{\alpha\beta}=\frac{\bar{\psi}\sigma^{\alpha\beta}\psi}{\bar{\psi}\psi},

(9.60)

which is simply
\bar{b}_{0}\sigma^{\alpha\beta}b_{0}
at zeroth order. Since
\bar{\psi}\sigma^{\alpha\beta}\psi
can be viewed as a spin density and
\bar{\psi}\psi
as a number density,
S^{\alpha\beta}
can be interpreted as the spin of a single particle.

Finally, we look at how the
u^{\mu}
curves deviate from being geodesics. We find that, to order
\hbar
,

\frac{Du^{\mu}}{D\tau}=u^{\nu}\nabla_{\nu}u^{\mu}=\frac{\hbar}{2mi}v^{\nu}\nabla_{\nu}\left(\nabla_{\mu}\bar{b}_{0}b_{0}-\bar{b}_{0}\nabla_{\mu}b_{0}\right).

(9.61)

So, using that for spinors, covariant derivatives commute as
[
82
]

\nabla_{[\mu}\nabla_{\nu]}\psi=\frac{i}{4}R_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}\sigma^{\alpha\beta}\psi,

(9.62)

and once again using (
9.55
) we have
